                              - text: This

                              - text: build absolutely decimates the endgame, being one of Warlocks overall best builds, that requires literally no gear to work exceptionally well. The damage is insane even competing with

                                  - generic [ref=f1060e483]:
                                    - generic "Weapon" [ref=f1060e484]
                                    - generic "Aggressive Aspect" [ref=f1060e486]
                                - generic [ref=f1060e493]:

                            - generic [ref=f1060e498]:
                              - generic [ref=f1060e499]: Equipment Priority
                              - button "Track Build" [ref=f1060e500] [cursor=pointer]
                            - button "Show All" [ref=f1060e549] [cursor=pointer]
                            - textbox [ref=f1060e558]:
                              - heading "This build doesn't rely on any items to get going, it's very strong right out of the gate, but here's the items that take it to the next level:" [level=3] [ref=f1060e559]
                              - list [ref=f1060e560]:
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e561]:
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e562]: Litany of Sable
                                  - text: is a pretty significant damage increase, simply just makes dread claws do more damage. It can be obtained by killing Astaroth in escalation nightmare dungeons, or crafting it via the Horadric Cube, which you can do my removing affixes on a 900 item power dagger to make it common, and then turning it into a unique
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e567]:
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e568]: Seed of Horazon
                                  - text: and
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e573]: Night Terror
                                  - text: are both very good amulet options and in good gear perform similarly.
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e578]: Night Terror
                                  - text: is better early game before you have a lot of gear with maximum resource on it, but the damage is more conditional and relies on you being in
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e583]: Terror Demon Metamorphosis
                                  - text: for full damage, which is why I personally prefer
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e588]: Seed of Horazon
                                  - text: "- you can use them interchangeably, and truthfully you should just use whatever amulet you happen to get a better roll of."
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e593]: Seed of Horazon
                                  - text: drops from Grigoire, and
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e598]: Night Terror
                                  - text: drops from Andariel
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e603]:
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e604]: Godslayer Crown
                                  - text: is entirely optional and just works well for speedfarming content, drops from Andariel as well
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e609]:
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e610]: Footfalls of the Waning World
                                  - text: are an incredible defensive option for us as it allows us to spam
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e615]: Nether Step
                                  - text: for huge damage reduction, in addition to no longer needing
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e620]: Nether Step
                                  - text: on our bars.
                                  - strong [ref=f1060e625]: These are entirely useless if they don't roll with +maximum evade charges or attacks reduce evade cooldown
                              - heading "Planner Fixes" [level=3] [ref=f1060e626]
                              - list [ref=f1060e627]:
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e628]: Overpower is actually Overpower per Stack.
                              - heading "Affix Priority" [level=3] [ref=f1060e629]
                              - list [ref=f1060e630]:
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e631]: Critical Strike Damage Multiplier
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e632]:
                                  - list [ref=f1060e633]:
                                    - listitem [ref=f1060e634]: It's by far our strongest affix once we get a high Critical Strike Chance
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e635]: Vulnerable Damage Multiplier
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e636]: All Damage/Shadow Damage Multiplier
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e637]: Attack Speed
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e638]: Willpower
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e639]: Weapon Damage
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e640]: Maximum LifeYou want at least 1 piece with vuln damage multi greater affix + MW crit > crit multi once you have really high crit multi due to diminishing returns.
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e641]: Armor
                              - heading "If you feel squishy prioritize more life/armor on your gear" [level=4] [ref=f1060e642]
                              - paragraph [ref=f1060e643]:
                                - text: Check out this
                                - strong [ref=f1060e644]
                                - link [ref=f1060e645] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1-QXvyDp0HjC01SRQGUmplTCK_n3eYeFY9AzJEE_QvEs/edit?gid=0#gid=0
                                  - strong [ref=f1060e646]: Sheet
                                - text: to balance out your Stats.
                              - heading "Transfigurations" [level=4] [ref=f1060e647]
                              - paragraph [ref=f1060e648]: For the love of god do not transfigure your items unless you are deep into endgame / 100% certain you won't need to change anything about that item again
                              - paragraph [ref=f1060e649]: I still don't know all of the possible transfigurations but here's my best estimation for BiS transfigures per item
                              - list [ref=f1060e650]:
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e651]:
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e652]: Aspect of Rallying Reversal
                                  - text: or
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e657]: Undying Aspect
                                  - text: Transfiguration on Amulet - Some people use
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e662]: Exploiter's Aspect
                                  - text: but I'm personally not a fan, even in pushing scenarios
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e667]:
                                  - text: For weapons the best transfigure is to try and get Gem Strength Multiplier on a 2her as this is a massive damage increase.
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e668]: The Grandfather
                                  - text: is very easy to acquire so it's a good candidate for trying to spam this until you get it, however it's a very low likelihood. It'll be better than
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e673]: Litany of Sable
                                  - text: + offhand if you get gem transfig and have flawless amethysts
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e678]: "Other good weapon options include: Crit Chance > Attack Speed > Quality > Shadow Damage Multi > % Max Life > Willpower"
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e679]:
                                  - generic [ref=f1060e680]: Heir of Perdition
                                  - text: try and get gem strength multi and put an Amethyst + Sapphire in it, otherwise Crit Chance > Attack Speed > Quality > SHadow Damage Multi > Willpower
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e685]: "Armor: Attack Speed > % Max Life > % Resistance > Quality > Shadow Damage Multi > Willpower"
                                - listitem [ref=f1060e686]: "Jewelry: +4 Occult/All Skills > Willpower > Quality > Resistance to all elements >"
                          - generic [ref=f1060e689]:
                            - generic [ref=f1060e690]:
                              - generic [ref=f1060e692]:

                              - paragraph [ref=f1060e963]:

  15  |       const xmlData = await response.text();

  17  |       const arrayLinks = Array.from(xmlData.matchAll(linkRegex));
